# RedHat_Management local defaults/env.yml variable guide

This file documents variables in defaults/env.yml for standalone project use.

- Keep defaults/env.yml encrypted with ansible-vault.
- Vault password file is expected at ~/.ansible/conf/.defaults_env.vaultpass.txt by default.

## Variables

| Variable | Synopsis | More info |
|---|---|---|
| AUTOMATION_HUB_URL | Enter value for redhat management AUTOMATION HUB URL (RedHat_Management) | https://access.redhat.com/management/api |
| AWSINFO | Enter value for redhat management AWSINFO (RedHat_Management) | https://access.redhat.com/management/api |
| AWSINFO_zone_id | Enter value for redhat management AWSINFO zone id (RedHat_Management) | https://access.redhat.com/management/api |
